+"""Display, probes and saved artifacts use the geometry the solve ran on.
+
+A run-time (or capacity-workflow) imperfection moves the reference coordinates
+of a copy of the model; the displacements are measured from that copy.  The
+deformed shape must therefore be "imperfect + displacement", and a saved
+artifact must carry that reference so reopening it draws the same shape.
+Runs without an imperfection, and project imperfections applied at build time,
+keep drawing on the mesh coordinates bit for bit.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import numpy as np
+import pytest
+
+from anyfem import eigenmode_imperfection, solve_capacity, solve_nonlinear_static
+from anyfem.io.artifacts import ArtifactStore
+from anyfem.io.result_artifact import write_solution_artifact
+from anyfem.ui.scene import build_persisted_result_scene, build_result_scene
+
+from test_imperfect_solved_geometry import (  # noqa: F401
+    AMPLITUDE,
+    compressed_plate,
+    plate_and_mode,
+)
+
+
+def _fe_coordinates(model, node_ids):
+    return np.array([[n.x, n.y, n.z] for n in (model.mesh.get_node(i) for i in node_ids)])
+
+
+def _scene_positions(scene):
+    return np.concatenate(
+        [np.asarray(p, dtype=float).reshape(-1, 3) for patch in scene.faces for p in patch.polygons]
+    )
+
+
+def _runtime(plate_and_mode):
+    project, mesh, buckling = plate_and_mode
+    imperfection = eigenmode_imperfection(buckling, 1, amplitude=AMPLITUDE)
+    return solve_nonlinear_static(
+        project, mesh=mesh, imperfection=imperfection, max_load_factor=1.0, num_steps=1,
+    )
+
+
+def _capacity(plate_and_mode):
+    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
+    return solve_capacity(
+        project, mesh=mesh, num_buckling_modes=1, imperfection_amplitude=AMPLITUDE,
+        num_steps=1, max_load_factor=1.0,
+    )
+
+
+def _store(tmp_path, solution):
+    project = solution.built.project
+    store = ArtifactStore(tmp_path / "plate.anyfem")
+    identity = dict(mesh_id="mesh", document_id=project.document_id,
+                    model_hash="model", mesh_hash="hash")
+    store.write_mesh(solution.built.mesh, **identity)
+    artifact = write_solution_artifact(store, solution, job_id="job", analysis_hash="a", **identity)
+    return store, artifact
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("route", ["nonlinear", "capacity"])
+def test_deformed_positions_are_imperfect_reference_plus_displacement(plate_and_mode, route):
+    solution = (_runtime if route == "nonlinear" else _capacity)(plate_and_mode)
+    ids = sorted(solution.built.mesh.nodes)
+    reference = _fe_coordinates(solution.solved_model(), ids)
+    assert np.max(np.abs(reference - solution.built.mesh.node_positions())) > 0.5 * AMPLITUDE
+
+    np.testing.assert_array_equal(solution.reference_positions(), reference)
+    expected = reference + 3.0 * solution.translations()
+    np.testing.assert_array_equal(solution.deformed_positions(3.0), expected)
+
+    scene = build_result_scene(solution, field="magnitude", scale=3.0)
+    by_node = dict(zip(ids, expected))
+    first = next(iter(solution.built.mesh.shells))
+    corners = np.array([by_node[n] for n in solution.built.mesh.corners_of(first)])
+    polygons = [np.asarray(poly) for patch in scene.faces for poly in patch.polygons]
+    assert any(poly.shape == corners.shape and np.array_equal(poly, corners) for poly in polygons)
+
+
+def test_csv_positions_use_the_reference_geometry(plate_and_mode):
+    from anyfem.post.report import field_to_csv
+
+    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
+    ids = sorted(solution.built.mesh.nodes)
+    reference = dict(zip(ids, _fe_coordinates(solution.solved_model(), ids)))
+    rows = [line.split(",") for line in field_to_csv(solution, "magnitude").splitlines()[1:]]
+    assert rows
+    for row in rows:
+        np.testing.assert_allclose([float(v) for v in row[1:4]], reference[int(row[0])], atol=1e-8)
+
+
+def test_artifact_records_the_reference_geometry_and_reopens_on_it(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
+    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
+    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, solution)
+    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)
+    ids = sorted(solution.built.mesh.nodes)
+    reference = _fe_coordinates(solution.solved_model(), ids)
+
+    assert dataset.metadata("provenance")["reference_geometry"]["kind"] == "solved_imperfect"
+    np.testing.assert_array_equal(np.asarray(dataset.table("reference_node_ids")), ids)
+    np.testing.assert_array_equal(np.asarray(dataset.table("reference_node_positions")), reference)
+
+    scene = build_persisted_result_scene(
+        solution.built.project, solution.built.mesh, dataset, "displacement", scale=3.0
+    )
+    live = build_result_scene(solution, field="magnitude", scale=3.0)
+    np.testing.assert_array_equal(_scene_positions(scene), _scene_positions(live))
+
+
+def test_ordinary_artifact_names_the_mesh_and_gains_no_table(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
+    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
+    solution = solve_nonlinear_static(project, mesh=mesh, max_load_factor=0.5, num_steps=1)
+    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, solution)
+    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)
+    assert dataset.metadata("provenance")["reference_geometry"]["kind"] == "mesh_record"
+    assert "reference_node_positions" not in dataset.table_keys
+    scene = build_persisted_result_scene(
+        project, solution.built.mesh, dataset, "displacement", scale=3.0
+    )
+    live = build_result_scene(solution, field="magnitude", scale=3.0)
+    np.testing.assert_array_equal(_scene_positions(scene), _scene_positions(live))
+
+
+def test_artifact_without_a_reference_record_reads_as_the_mesh(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
+    from anyfem.ui.scene import persisted_reference_positions
+
+    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
+    solution = solve_nonlinear_static(project, mesh=mesh, max_load_factor=0.5, num_steps=1)
+    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, solution)
+    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)
+
+    class Old:  # an artifact written before the reference record existed
+        table_keys = tuple(k for k in dataset.table_keys if not k.startswith("reference_"))
+        table = staticmethod(dataset.table)
+
+        @staticmethod
+        def metadata(name):
+            old = dict(dataset.metadata(name))
+            old.pop("reference_geometry", None)
+            return old
+
+    ordered = sorted(mesh.nodes)
+    np.testing.assert_array_equal(
+        persisted_reference_positions(Old, mesh, ordered), mesh.node_positions()
+    )
+
+
+def test_unbound_runtime_imperfection_refuses_the_display(plate_and_mode):
+    from anyfem.post.results import NonlinearSolution, UnboundSolvedGeometryError
+
+    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
+    unbound = NonlinearSolution(
+        displacements=solution.displacements, built=solution.built,
+        raw_result=solution.raw_result, info=dict(solution.info),
+    )
+    with pytest.raises(UnboundSolvedGeometryError):
+        unbound.deformed_positions(1.0)
+
+
+def test_ordinary_and_build_time_displays_are_unchanged(plate_and_mode):
+    from anyfem.model.imperfections import Imperfection
+
+    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
+    plain = solve_nonlinear_static(project, mesh=mesh, max_load_factor=0.5, num_steps=1)
+    np.testing.assert_array_equal(
+        plain.deformed_positions(2.0), mesh.node_positions() + 2.0 * plain.translations()
+    )
+    p2, face = compressed_plate()
+    p2.imperfections.append(Imperfection(name="dent", ref=p2.face(face), amplitude=AMPLITUDE,
+                                         kind="plate_mode", direction=(0.0, 0.0, 1.0)))
+    m2 = p2.generate_mesh(0.25)
+    built_time = solve_nonlinear_static(p2, mesh=m2, max_load_factor=0.5, num_steps=1)
+    # Pinned scope: build-time imperfections keep drawing on the mesh record.
+    np.testing.assert_array_equal(
+        built_time.deformed_positions(2.0), m2.node_positions() + 2.0 * built_time.translations()
+    )
+
+
+def test_artifact_of_an_unbound_imperfect_result_refuses_its_reference(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
+    from anyfem.post.results import NonlinearSolution
+
+    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
+    unbound = NonlinearSolution(
+        displacements=solution.displacements, built=solution.built,
+        raw_result=solution.raw_result, info=dict(solution.info),
+    )
+    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, unbound)
+    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)
+    assert dataset.metadata("provenance")["reference_geometry"]["kind"] == "unbound_imperfect"
+    assert "reference_node_positions" not in dataset.table_keys
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="does not store"):
+        build_persisted_result_scene(
+            unbound.built.project, unbound.built.mesh, dataset, "displacement", scale=1.0
+        )

# ANY Ecosystem Philosophy

## Canonical ownership

Each domain has one authoritative owner and dependencies flow downstream:

- ANYgeometry owns geometry, topology, persistent identity, tolerance,
  spatial indexing, intersections, and geometry audit truth.
- ANYmesh owns discretization and consumes qualified geometry contracts.
- ANYmaterial owns material definitions, validation, and canonical conventions.
- ANYio/ANYfileio owns interchange syntax and neutral file semantics.
- ANYfeSolver owns solver physics, numerical kernels, and qualification scope.
- ANYfem owns FEM workflow, projects, orchestration, and postprocessing.
- UI/application repositories consume public APIs and do not recreate domain truth.

Copied owner models, reverse imports, and coordinate-derived replacements for
owner topology are architectural defects. Compatibility facades may exist only
as thin, explicit, tested aliases.

## Contract doctrine

1. Prefer headless, public, stable contracts. Integration and UI layers use
   public APIs rather than private helpers.
2. Fail closed. Unsupported, unqualified, stale, wrong-model, or ambiguous
   inputs produce typed non-success; downstream preserves that truth.
3. Do not invent, narrow, or silently discard semantics. Preserve unknown
   interchange records, absent components, provenance, material symmetry, and
   solver-reported status.
4. Identity is model-bound and persistent. IDs are not guessed from coordinates,
   silently reused, or detached from revision/lineage.
5. Public mutation is atomic and rollback-safe. Incremental work follows explicit
   change sets and invalidation, not tolerance-based global rediscovery.
6. Boundary conventions are ecosystem contracts: SI internally, conversion once
   at ingestion/display, canonical material Voigt order, and structural protocols
   rather than concrete cross-package inheritance.

## Evidence and qualification

- Source and tests are behavioral truth. Reports are commit-, command-, and
  environment-scoped evidence, not timeless capability declarations.
- Qualification is narrower than implementation. A downstream application cannot
  broaden the solver owner's qualified scope.
- Partial runs, skipped execution, XFAIL, deck generation, and `not_executed` are
  never reported as passes.
- Failure evidence is retained. Plausible estimates, zeros, or fallbacks never
  replace a real non-success result.
- Every capability claim names the exact implementation identity, source SHA,
  contract version, command, result, platform, and remaining limits.

## Performance doctrine

Correctness and sound theory rank above performance. Implementations must be
physics-first where physics is involved and independently verifiable. Artificial
or hidden penalties, empirical tuning, invented stiffness, and other â€œmagicâ€
corrections may not be introduced merely to satisfy a numerical or performance
gate. A derived formulation requires an explicit identity, derivation,
provenance, and qualification.

Performance cannot change semantics. Every acceleration keeps a correctness
oracle, explicit eligibility, lifecycle, invalidation, deterministic diagnostics,
and fail-closed fallback rules. Timing alone does not prove activation or
correctness.

No production stage may retain demonstrated global-quadratic behavior where the
governing plan requires subquadratic scaling. Memory evidence distinguishes
Python-traced allocation from process RSS and native allocation. Performance
qualification follows the effective workspace resource policy and any task-specific
resource limits. In Normal-token mode, worker-owned scheduling applies under
`C:/Github/AGENTS.md`; historical lease rules do not reactivate themselves.

## Long-term stance

Prefer additive migration paths, explicit versions, and reversible compatibility.
Retire duplicated ownership rather than institutionalizing it. Frozen historical
trees remain historical and never regain authority through convenience imports.

## Adopted engineering and delivery policy

The following revision governs future development procedure across the ecosystem.
It supplements the ownership and technical doctrine above. Existing scientific,
release and task-specific execution boundaries remain in force as specified below.

### Risk-Proportionate Engineering and Delivery

**Policy ID:** `ANY_ECOSYSTEM_RISK_PROPORTIONATE_ENGINEERING_V1`

**Date:** 2026-09-24

**Status:** Adopted by user direction, effective 2026-09-24. Revision: `2026-09-24.2`. Canonical home: `ANYopenSoft/governance/ECOSYSTEM_PHILOSOPHY.md`.

**Scope:** All ANY packages, applications, research tools, integration workflows and participating human/AI development agents, including ECOboss coordination where used.

> **Develop iteratively, verify throughout, qualify the intended use, and preserve the evidence supporting the claim. Apply rigor to consequential failure modes; do not turn every development experiment into a qualification ceremony.**

### 1. Purpose and unchanged principles

Deliver useful, trustworthy engineering workflows. Measure progress by a working capability, a resolved uncertainty or a demonstrated improvementâ€”not by the number of plans, tests, agents, review rounds or archived files.

Scientific correctness, numerical honesty, user-data integrity and safe execution remain non-negotiable. Performance, lightweight deployment, maintainability, accessible interfaces and reproducibility remain primary design objectives. Optimize measured end-to-end work without silently changing the model, accuracy, admissible domain or reported meaning of results.

Preserve package ownership and interfaces. Reuse existing numerical kernels, transaction stores, geometry/topology services, material definitions, file formats and command layers before introducing competing infrastructure. Keep the open engineering packages independently useful; this update does not change any license or the boundary of proprietary products.

Scope assurance by **consequence, technical uncertainty, exposure and intended claim**, not by filename or project size. A research oracle, result-unit conversion or GUI command can be as consequential as a numerical kernel. Conversely, an isolated presentation change does not require a mechanics qualification programme.

This policy changes how development is organized. It does not itself qualify software, authorize a release, broaden an existing capability, waive a scientific requirement or override an explicit user restriction.

### 2. Assess risk and maturity separately

At the start of a substantial task, record the intended outcome, the consequential failure modes and the required acceptance evidence in the existing task note or pull request. A few sentences normally suffice; no new risk-scoring application or mandatory numerical risk score is required.

| Change risk | Typical examples | Proportionate assurance |
|---|---|---|
| Low, local and readily reversible | Editorial wording or layout with no change to numerical meaning, permissions or stored data | Normal review/CI and focused checks. No scientific acceptance dossier. |
| Behavioral or cross-package | New adapters, workflows, APIs, packaging or performance paths | Focused regression and end-to-end tests, compatibility review and honest capability documentation. Escalate where errors can silently change engineering results. |
| High consequence or difficult to detect | Mechanics, constitutive histories, units/frames, topology identity, constraints, numerical reference tools, uncertainty/fatigue calculations, restart, contact history, destructive migrations or privileged AI commands | Early risk-focused technical review, independent verification appropriate to the claim, and controlled acceptance before the changed behavior is relied upon. |

These examples are not automatic classifications. Reassess when exposure, dependencies, supported combinations or the proposed claim change. Explain a downgrade; do not relabel high-risk work merely to avoid a gate. Formal external obligations remain applicable.

Maturity is a separate dimension:

| Level | Purpose and permitted use | Minimum record and review |
|---|---|---|
| **A â€” Development and investigation** | Explore, implement, refactor, diagnose and run authorized integrated experiments. Results are provisional and cannot confer qualification. | Recoverable source, relevant tests, commands/inputs needed to reproduce important findings, and normal change review. Add early independent technical scrutiny for high-risk assumptions; do not require a formal acceptance package for every helper. |
| **B â€” Candidate acceptance** | Establish a named scientific, numerical, interoperability or performance claim within a declared domain. | Prospective criteria, exact candidate identity, controlled relevant runs, retained raw evidence and an independent acceptance assessment. Include a pre-run review when a governing contract or the risk requires it. |
| **C â€” Supported delivery or release** | Make the admitted capability available through a supported interface, artifact, default or release. | Impact-appropriate regression/qualification, matching installed-artifact checks, compatibility, recovery/restart/resource checks where applicable, release notes and the existing approval process. Reuse valid B evidence rather than regenerate it automatically. |

A routine low-risk patch does not need an artificial Level-B scientific study before release. High-risk development does not become low-risk because it lives under `scripts/`, is opt-in or is labelled experimental. Packaging, merge status, public exposure and scientific qualification remain distinct decisions.

### 3. Default development loop

Use the shortest loop that can establish the next meaningful result:

```text
Define the outcome and important failure modes
    -> inspect/reuse existing implementation and evidence
    -> implement a bounded end-to-end slice
    -> run local and integration checks
    -> investigate discrepancies with targeted independent checks
    -> retain the useful result or reject the approach
    -> repeat until a claim-bearing candidate is ready
Claude API credits are exhausted. Do not invoke Claude through OpenCode's Anthropic provider, direct API calls, API-key-authenticated Claude CLI, LiteLLM or another proxy until the user explicitly re-enables API use. Do not probe exhausted credits or silently switch billing routes/providers. Local OpenCode remains available through its queued loopback wrapper; other cloud providers require explicit authorisation.

Use the installed Claude CLI, normally `$HOME\.local\bin\claude.exe`, with the user's model choice; default `claude-sonnet-5-5`. The local executable is a runner for cloud subscription inference, not a local model. Local-only data restrictions still apply.

Before inference, create a child-process-only environment without `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, `ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN`, `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` or other API/proxy/provider overrides. Exclude API-key helpers and third-party provider routing through invocation-specific settings. Preserve the parent environment, persistent settings and credentials. Run `claude auth status` under the same effective authentication settings; require `loggedIn: true`, `authMethod: claude.ai` and the expected subscription. Never print credentials or unnecessary account identifiers. If subscription authentication is unavailable or ambiguous, use OpenAI rather than API fallback; report that limitation. Do not use `--bare`, which excludes subscription OAuth in this installation, or initiate login/account changes without authorisation.

Run noninteractively from the explicit owned repository root. Supply an invocation-specific settings file with hooks/plugins disabled and scoped tool permissions; use `--setting-sources ''`, `--strict-mcp-config` and `--disable-slash-commands` to exclude unrelated settings, MCP servers and skills. Check effective auth/routing/permissions before tool-enabled work. Expose only necessary tools with `--tools`; allow exact Read inspections/check commands and owned Edit paths. Read denies editing; both modes deny nested agents, network tools, unauthorised external paths and Git integration except explicit Astra authority. Fable and other Claude CLI workers can read local files/directories through explicitly granted Read tools; grant necessary additional local directories explicitly rather than describing the worker as unable to access local files. Never use permission-bypass/auto-approval modes to overcome a failure. Application permissions are not an OS sandbox; fall back if scope cannot be enforced.

```powershell
# After preparing the subscription-only child environment and scoped settings:
& "$HOME\.local\bin\claude.exe" --print --model "<user model; default claude-sonnet-5-5>" `
    --setting-sources '' --settings "<invocation-specific settings JSON path>" `
    --strict-mcp-config --disable-slash-commands --tools "<minimal tool list>" `
    --output-format json "<bounded Read/Edit brief>"
```

For Read reviews, use the shared subscription-only Windows launcher below instead of inline subprocess wrappers. It defaults to Sonnet 5.5; pass `--model fable` when requested. Its 600-second default review deadline reflects substantial reviews that have taken over 270 seconds; choose a different bound explicitly for the task and remaining authority, without renewing numerical/check budgets. Read access defaults to the repository: narrow it with repeated `--read-path` grants. For an authorised directory outside the repository, supply both `--add-dir` and its `--read-path`; this grants read access, not edits or commands. The launcher exposes only Read, blocks hooks/plugins/MCP/nested workers/shell/network tools, and requires subscription authentication.

```powershell
python "$HOME\bin\claude-review.py" --repo "<absolute repository root>" `
    --brief-file "<review brief file>" --output-directory "<new evidence directory outside source roots>" `
    --model "<user model; default claude-sonnet-5-5>" --timeout-seconds 600 `
    --read-path "<authorised file or directory>"
# Add --add-dir "<authorised additional directory>" and a matching --read-path when needed.
```

Use streaming JSON with partial messages; flush full stdout/stderr to disk during execution. Report compact progress at most every 30 seconds and track last event/first response and tool counts; progress is not advice or acceptance, and silence alone is not proof of failure. Preserve partial output on timeout. The shared launcher assigns the suspended process to a kill-on-close Windows Job Object before allowing execution, terminates its owned process tree on timeout/interruption/exit, verifies zero remaining owned processes and always saves a terminal `result.json` once execution evidence is created. Cleanup errors, startup failure, API errors, missing final advice and timeout must be distinct non-success outcomes; a zero exit alone does not establish a completed review. Do not silently restart an explicitly one-time review or kill unrelated processes. For Edit work retain the scoped CLI route above with an equally reliable supervisor; the Read launcher does not grant Edit authority. Capture full results/stderr outside the repository and inspect actual tool outcomes, errors and handoff state. Retain session identity only when useful for same-owner continuation; otherwise use `--no-session-persistence`. CLI installation, subscription auth status and a text-only answer do not establish tool capability: confirm it through a useful bounded Read assignment when needed. All ownership, resource, checkpoint, independent review and acceptance rules remain binding.

## Antigravity CLI workers

Antigravity CLI is an authorised optional worker route using its existing login, separate from Claude CLI subscription, direct Claude API credits, Mistral Vibe and Batch API access. Do not change accounts/billing, supply API-key overrides or silently switch routes. This authorisation does not re-enable exhausted direct Claude API routes or bypass local-only data restrictions. Executable: `agy` on PATH, normally `$HOME\AppData\Local\agy\bin\agy.exe`. Default model `claude-opus-5-5-medium`, effort `medium`; honour explicit user choices and check `agy models` only when the selected model needs resolving.

Run from the explicit owned repository root with a concise Read/Edit brief. Inspect and enforce scoped agent/tool permissions before repository work; grant only needed local reads, owned Edit paths and authorised checks. Use `--sandbox`, disable slash commands, and deny nested workers, unrelated directories, network tools and Git integration unless explicitly authorised. Read uses plan mode; Edit requires express ownership and compatible scoped permissions. Plan mode and the sandbox flag alone do not prove read-only/file-scope enforcement. If scope cannot be enforced, use a constrained alternative rather than permissive defaults. Never use `--dangerously-skip-permissions` or auto mode to bypass a denial.

```powershell
# Run from the owned root after establishing Read scope and permissions:
& "$HOME\AppData\Local\agy\bin\agy.exe" `
    --model claude-opus-5-5-medium --effort medium --mode plan --sandbox `
    --disable-slash-commands --print-timeout 600s --output-format stream-json `

"""The reference geometry a nonlinear solve actually ran on.

Project imperfections are applied while the model is built, so they already
live in ``built.fe_model``.  A run-time ``imperfection`` passed to the
nonlinear-static or arc-length solve, and the capacity workflow's eigenmode
imperfection, are applied by the solver to a *copy* of that model.  The
displacements are measured from the copy's coordinates, so every recovery
that combines them with geometry -- element frames, strain-displacement
operators, transverse shear -- has to use the copy as well.  Recovering on the
perfect model mixes two geometries and silently returns wrong stresses.

:class:`SolvedGeometry` binds a result to that solved model.  It is exact or
it refuses:

* the capacity workflow returns the very model it solved, which is kept as is;
* for nonlinear static and arc length the solver keeps its copy to itself.
  ANYfem converts the imperfection once into an owned snapshot, hands that
  snapshot to the solver, checks it against the solver's provenance record
  after the solve, and immediately makes the same copy through the solver's
  own :func:`apply_imperfection` (bit-identical coordinates, and the model
  state as it was when solved).  A disagreement is a typed refusal, not a
  plausible number.

The copy costs one model deep copy after an imperfect solve; runs without an
imperfection pay nothing.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, List, Mapping, Optional

import numpy as np

from ..model.project import ProjectError

__all__ = [
    "SolvedGeometry",
    "UnboundSolvedGeometryError",
    "owned_imperfection_field",
    "reference_position_map",
    "solved_reference_positions",
    "solved_model_of",
    "unbound_refusal",
]


class UnboundSolvedGeometryError(ProjectError):
    """A result's solved geometry is unknown or disagrees with its solver record."""


def _trusted_imperfection(imperfection: Any) -> bool:
    """Plain data or the solver's own pure converters, checked by exact type.

    Only these are converted outside the solver.  A caller-defined converter,
    or a subclass of a mapping or field, may run code, return different
    offsets on another call or mutate the model; it is therefore handed to the
    solver unchanged so the solver's own dispatch, validation and mutation
    guard still apply -- and its geometry is not reproduced for recovery.
    """

    from anyfesolver.imperfections import (
        CompositeImperfection,
        EigenmodeImperfection,
        ImperfectionField,
        StandardImperfection,
    )

    # Trust is decided by exact built-in types all the way down, without
    # iterating anything that is not a built-in container: a custom mapping,
    # sequence or array-like can run code when read, and a one-shot iterator
    # would be consumed by the check itself.
    kind = type(imperfection)
    if kind is dict:
        return _plain_offsets(imperfection)
    if kind is ImperfectionField:
        return _plain_offsets(imperfection.offsets) and type(imperfection.metadata) is dict
    if kind is EigenmodeImperfection:
        return type(imperfection.buckling_result).__module__.startswith("anyfesolver.")
    if kind is StandardImperfection:
        return type(imperfection.node_ids) in (tuple, list) and all(
            type(node) is int for node in imperfection.node_ids
        )
    if kind is CompositeImperfection:
        return type(imperfection.components) in (tuple, list) and all(
            _trusted_imperfection(part) for part in imperfection.components
        )
    return False


_PLAIN_NUMBERS = (int, float, np.float64, np.float32, np.int64, np.int32)


def _plain_offsets(offsets: Any) -> bool:
    if type(offsets) is not dict:
        return False
    for node, offset in offsets.items():
        if type(node) not in (int, np.int64, np.int32):
            return False
        if type(offset) is np.ndarray:
            if offset.dtype.kind not in "fiu":
                return False
        elif type(offset) in (tuple, list):
            if not all(type(value) in _PLAIN_NUMBERS for value in offset):
                return False
        else:
            return False
    return True


def owned_imperfection_field(base_model: Any, imperfection: Any):
    """An immutable snapshot of ``imperfection`` as the solver will see it.

    Returns ``None`` for an input that is not :func:`_trusted_imperfection`;
    the caller must then pass the original object to the solver and treat the
    solved geometry as not reproducible.  Otherwise the conversion follows the
    solver's own owned-input snapshot: a plain mapping is the offsets (name
    ``"imperfection"``, no metadata), a field keeps its name and metadata, and
    a converter is called once against ``base_model`` -- the model state the
    solver converts against.  Offsets become float64 copies keyed by integer
    node id.
    """

    from anyfesolver.imperfections import ImperfectionField

    if not _trusted_imperfection(imperfection):
        return None
    if type(imperfection) is dict:
        offsets, name, metadata = imperfection, "imperfection", {}
    else:
        field = (
            imperfection
            if type(imperfection) is ImperfectionField
            else imperfection.to_field(base_model)
        )
        if type(field) is not ImperfectionField or not isinstance(field.offsets, Mapping) \
                or not isinstance(field.metadata, Mapping):
            return None
        offsets, name, metadata = field.offsets, field.name, field.metadata
    owned = {
        int(node_id): np.asarray(offset, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
        for node_id, offset in offsets.items()
    }
    return ImperfectionField(owned, name=str(name), metadata=dict(metadata))


def _has_offset(imperfection_field: Any) -> bool:
    # ``!= 0`` is also true for NaN, so a non-finite field is never treated as
    # the perfect geometry.
    return any(np.any(offset != 0.0) for offset in imperfection_field.offsets.values())


def _records(entries: Any) -> List[Mapping[str, Any]]:
    if entries is None:
        return []
    return [entry for entry in entries if isinstance(entry, Mapping)]


def _same_record(left: Mapping[str, Any], right: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
    return (
        str(left.get("name")) == str(right.get("name"))
        and float(left.get("max_offset", np.nan)) == float(right.get("max_offset", np.nan))
    )


def _solver_imperfection_records(raw_result: Any) -> Optional[List[Mapping[str, Any]]]:
    info = getattr(raw_result, "info", None)
    if not isinstance(info, Mapping) or "imperfection" not in info:
        return None
    return _records(info.get("imperfection"))


class SolvedGeometry:
    """The FE model a result's displacements are measured from, or a refusal."""

    __slots__ = ("_model", "_refusal")

    def __init__(self, *, model: Any = None, refusal: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
        if (model is None) == (refusal is None):
            raise ValueError("SolvedGeometry needs exactly one of a model or a refusal")
        self._model = model
        self._refusal = refusal

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @classmethod
    def exact(cls, model: Any) -> "SolvedGeometry":
        """Bind to the model object the solver returned as solved."""

        return cls(model=model)

    @classmethod
    def refused(cls, reason: str) -> "SolvedGeometry":
        """A binding that refuses recovery, e.g. for views of an unbound result."""

        return cls(refusal=str(reason))

    @classmethod
    def rebuild(cls, base_model: Any, imperfection_field: Any, raw_result: Any,
                *, context: str) -> Optional["SolvedGeometry"]:
        """Bind to ``base_model`` plus the run-time imperfection the solver applied.

        Call this straight after the solve, with the owned snapshot from
        :func:`owned_imperfection_field` that was also the object handed to the
        solver -- so solve and recovery consume one conversion.  The copy is
        made here, eagerly, so later edits to the built model (coordinates,
        thickness, materials, connectivity) cannot leak into it: it is the same
        deep copy of the same model state the solver made, moved by the same
        offsets through the same :func:`apply_imperfection`.  Returns ``None``
        when every offset is exactly zero: the solved coordinates are then the
        base coordinates bit for bit, and the ordinary route stays untouched.
        """

        from anyfesolver.imperfections import ImperfectionField

        if not isinstance(imperfection_field, ImperfectionField):
            raise TypeError("rebuild needs the owned ImperfectionField given to the solver")
        records = _solver_imperfection_records(raw_result)
        build_records = _records(getattr(base_model, "imperfection_metadata", None))
        expected = {"name": imperfection_field.name, "max_offset": imperfection_field.max_offset}
        if (
            records is None
            or len(records) != len(build_records) + 1
            or not all(_same_record(a, b) for a, b in zip(records, build_records))
            or not _same_record(records[-1], expected)
        ):
            raise UnboundSolvedGeometryError(
                f"{context}: the solver's imperfection record {records!r} does not "
                f"match the build-time imperfections {build_records!r} plus the "
                f"run-time imperfection {expected!r}; the solved geometry cannot be "
                "reproduced, so no stresses are recovered on it"
            )
        if not _has_offset(imperfection_field):
            return None
        from anyfesolver import apply_imperfection

        return cls(model=apply_imperfection(base_model, imperfection_field, copy_model=True))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def model(self) -> Any:
        """The solved FE model; raises when this binding is a refusal."""

        if self._refusal is not None:
            raise UnboundSolvedGeometryError(self._refusal)
        return self._model

    def __getstate__(self):
        return (self._model, self._refusal)

    def __setstate__(self, state) -> None:
        self._model, self._refusal = state


def _runtime_imperfection_unbound(view: Any) -> Optional[str]:
    records = _solver_imperfection_records(getattr(view, "raw_result", None))
    if records is not None:
        build_records = _records(getattr(view.built.fe_model, "imperfection_metadata", None))
        runtime = records[len(build_records):]
        if any(float(entry.get("max_offset", np.nan)) != 0.0 for entry in runtime):
            return "a run-time imperfection"
    info = getattr(view, "info", None)
    workflow = info.get("raw") if isinstance(info, Mapping) else None
    imperfect = getattr(workflow, "imperfect_model", None)
    if imperfect is not None and imperfect is not view.built.fe_model:
        field = getattr(workflow, "imperfection", None)
        if field is None or _has_offset(field):
            return "the capacity workflow's imperfection"
    return None


def unbound_refusal(view: Any) -> Optional[str]:
    """The refusal message for an imperfect result without a binding, if any."""

    reason = _runtime_imperfection_unbound(view)
    if reason is None:
        return None
    return (
        f"this result was solved on geometry moved by {reason}, but it carries "
        "no binding to that solved model; stresses recovered on the perfect "
        "built model would be wrong, so recovery is refused"
    )


def solved_model_of(view: Any) -> Any:
    """The FE model to recover ``view``'s displacements on.

    Views without a binding use ``built.fe_model``, exactly as before -- unless
    their solver record says the solve ran on imperfect geometry that is not
    in ``built.fe_model``, which is refused rather than recovered on the
    perfect shape.
    """

    geometry = getattr(view, "solved_geometry", None)
    if geometry is not None:
        return geometry.model()
    refusal = unbound_refusal(view)
    if refusal is not None:
        raise UnboundSolvedGeometryError(refusal)
    return view.built.fe_model


def solved_reference_positions(view: Any) -> Optional[np.ndarray]:
    """Reference coordinates of a result solved on moved geometry, else ``None``.

    ``None`` means the ordinary route: the mesh coordinates are the reference,
    untouched.  Otherwise the rows follow ``sorted(built.mesh.nodes)`` and come
    from the bound solved model; a refused or missing binding raises.
    """

    geometry = getattr(view, "solved_geometry", None)
    if geometry is None:
        refusal = unbound_refusal(view)
        if refusal is not None:
            raise UnboundSolvedGeometryError(refusal)
        return None
    model = geometry.model()
    get_node = model.mesh.get_node
    rows = []
    for node_id in sorted(view.built.mesh.nodes):
        node = get_node(node_id)
        if node is None:
            raise UnboundSolvedGeometryError(
                f"the solved model has no node {node_id}; its geometry does not match the mesh"
            )
        rows.append((node.x, node.y, node.z))
    return np.asarray(rows, dtype=float).reshape(-1, 3)


def reference_position_map(view: Any, mesh: Any) -> Any:
    """``node id -> xyz`` of the reference geometry: ``mesh.nodes`` unless moved."""

    positions = solved_reference_positions(view)
    if positions is None:
        return mesh.nodes
    return {node_id: positions[row] for row, node_id in enumerate(sorted(mesh.nodes))}
"""Display, probes and saved artifacts use the geometry the solve ran on.

A run-time (or capacity-workflow) imperfection moves the reference coordinates
of a copy of the model; the displacements are measured from that copy.  The
deformed shape must therefore be "imperfect + displacement", and a saved
artifact must carry that reference so reopening it draws the same shape.
Runs without an imperfection, and project imperfections applied at build time,
keep drawing on the mesh coordinates bit for bit.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from anyfem import eigenmode_imperfection, solve_capacity, solve_nonlinear_static
from anyfem.io.artifacts import ArtifactStore
from anyfem.io.result_artifact import write_solution_artifact
from anyfem.ui.scene import build_persisted_result_scene, build_result_scene

from test_imperfect_solved_geometry import (  # noqa: F401
    AMPLITUDE,
    compressed_plate,
    plate_and_mode,
)


def _fe_coordinates(model, node_ids):
    return np.array([[n.x, n.y, n.z] for n in (model.mesh.get_node(i) for i in node_ids)])


def _scene_positions(scene):
    return np.concatenate(
        [np.asarray(p, dtype=float).reshape(-1, 3) for patch in scene.faces for p in patch.polygons]
    )


def _runtime(plate_and_mode):
    project, mesh, buckling = plate_and_mode
    imperfection = eigenmode_imperfection(buckling, 1, amplitude=AMPLITUDE)
    return solve_nonlinear_static(
        project, mesh=mesh, imperfection=imperfection, max_load_factor=1.0, num_steps=1,
    )


def _capacity(plate_and_mode):
    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
    return solve_capacity(
        project, mesh=mesh, num_buckling_modes=1, imperfection_amplitude=AMPLITUDE,
        num_steps=1, max_load_factor=1.0,
    )


def _store(tmp_path, solution):
    project = solution.built.project
    store = ArtifactStore(tmp_path / "plate.anyfem")
    identity = dict(mesh_id="mesh", document_id=project.document_id,
                    model_hash="model", mesh_hash="hash")
    store.write_mesh(solution.built.mesh, **identity)
    artifact = write_solution_artifact(store, solution, job_id="job", analysis_hash="a", **identity)
    return store, artifact


@pytest.mark.parametrize("route", ["nonlinear", "capacity"])
def test_deformed_positions_are_imperfect_reference_plus_displacement(plate_and_mode, route):
    solution = (_runtime if route == "nonlinear" else _capacity)(plate_and_mode)
    ids = sorted(solution.built.mesh.nodes)
    reference = _fe_coordinates(solution.solved_model(), ids)
    assert np.max(np.abs(reference - solution.built.mesh.node_positions())) > 0.5 * AMPLITUDE

    np.testing.assert_array_equal(solution.reference_positions(), reference)
    expected = reference + 3.0 * solution.translations()
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(solution.deformed_positions(3.0), expected)

    scene = build_result_scene(solution, field="magnitude", scale=3.0)
    by_node = dict(zip(ids, expected))
    first = next(iter(solution.built.mesh.shells))
    corners = np.array([by_node[n] for n in solution.built.mesh.corners_of(first)])
    polygons = [np.asarray(poly) for patch in scene.faces for poly in patch.polygons]
    assert any(poly.shape == corners.shape and np.array_equal(poly, corners) for poly in polygons)


def test_csv_positions_use_the_reference_geometry(plate_and_mode):
    from anyfem.post.report import field_to_csv

    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
    ids = sorted(solution.built.mesh.nodes)
    reference = dict(zip(ids, _fe_coordinates(solution.solved_model(), ids)))
    rows = [line.split(",") for line in field_to_csv(solution, "magnitude").splitlines()[1:]]
    assert rows
    for row in rows:
        np.testing.assert_allclose([float(v) for v in row[1:4]], reference[int(row[0])], atol=1e-8)


def test_artifact_records_the_reference_geometry_and_reopens_on_it(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, solution)
    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)
    ids = sorted(solution.built.mesh.nodes)
    reference = _fe_coordinates(solution.solved_model(), ids)

    assert dataset.metadata("provenance")["reference_geometry"]["kind"] == "solved_imperfect"
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(np.asarray(dataset.table("reference_node_ids")), ids)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(np.asarray(dataset.table("reference_node_positions")), reference)

    scene = build_persisted_result_scene(
        solution.built.project, solution.built.mesh, dataset, "displacement", scale=3.0
    )
    live = build_result_scene(solution, field="magnitude", scale=3.0)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(_scene_positions(scene), _scene_positions(live))


def test_ordinary_artifact_names_the_mesh_and_gains_no_table(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
    solution = solve_nonlinear_static(project, mesh=mesh, max_load_factor=0.5, num_steps=1)
    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, solution)
    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)
    assert dataset.metadata("provenance")["reference_geometry"]["kind"] == "mesh_record"
    assert "reference_node_positions" not in dataset.table_keys
    scene = build_persisted_result_scene(
        project, solution.built.mesh, dataset, "displacement", scale=3.0
    )
    live = build_result_scene(solution, field="magnitude", scale=3.0)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(_scene_positions(scene), _scene_positions(live))


def test_artifact_without_a_reference_record_reads_as_the_mesh(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
    from anyfem.ui.scene import persisted_reference_positions

    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
    solution = solve_nonlinear_static(project, mesh=mesh, max_load_factor=0.5, num_steps=1)
    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, solution)
    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)

    class Old:  # an artifact written before the reference record existed
        table_keys = tuple(k for k in dataset.table_keys if not k.startswith("reference_"))
        table = staticmethod(dataset.table)

        @staticmethod
        def metadata(name):
            old = dict(dataset.metadata(name))
            old.pop("reference_geometry", None)
            return old

    ordered = sorted(mesh.nodes)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(
        persisted_reference_positions(Old, mesh, ordered), mesh.node_positions()
    )


def test_unbound_runtime_imperfection_refuses_the_display(plate_and_mode):
    from anyfem.post.results import NonlinearSolution, UnboundSolvedGeometryError

    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
    unbound = NonlinearSolution(
        displacements=solution.displacements, built=solution.built,
        raw_result=solution.raw_result, info=dict(solution.info),
    )
    with pytest.raises(UnboundSolvedGeometryError):
        unbound.deformed_positions(1.0)


def test_ordinary_and_build_time_displays_are_unchanged(plate_and_mode):
    from anyfem.model.imperfections import Imperfection

    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
    plain = solve_nonlinear_static(project, mesh=mesh, max_load_factor=0.5, num_steps=1)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(
        plain.deformed_positions(2.0), mesh.node_positions() + 2.0 * plain.translations()
    )
    p2, face = compressed_plate()
    p2.imperfections.append(Imperfection(name="dent", ref=p2.face(face), amplitude=AMPLITUDE,
                                         kind="plate_mode", direction=(0.0, 0.0, 1.0)))
    m2 = p2.generate_mesh(0.25)
    built_time = solve_nonlinear_static(p2, mesh=m2, max_load_factor=0.5, num_steps=1)
    # Pinned scope: build-time imperfections keep drawing on the mesh record.
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(
        built_time.deformed_positions(2.0), m2.node_positions() + 2.0 * built_time.translations()
    )


def test_artifact_of_an_unbound_imperfect_result_refuses_its_reference(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
    from anyfem.post.results import NonlinearSolution

    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
    unbound = NonlinearSolution(
        displacements=solution.displacements, built=solution.built,
        raw_result=solution.raw_result, info=dict(solution.info),

    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)
    assert dataset.metadata("provenance")["reference_geometry"]["kind"] == "unbound_imperfect"

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="does not store"):
        build_persisted_result_scene(
            unbound.built.project, unbound.built.mesh, dataset, "displacement", scale=1.0
        )

def _has_offset(imperfection_field: Any) -> bool:
    # ``!= 0`` is also true for NaN, so a non-finite field is never treated as
    # the perfect geometry.
    return any(np.any(offset != 0.0) for offset in imperfection_field.offsets.values())


def _records(entries: Any) -> List[Mapping[str, Any]]:
    if entries is None:
        return []
    return [entry for entry in entries if isinstance(entry, Mapping)]


def _same_record(left: Mapping[str, Any], right: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
    return (
        str(left.get("name")) == str(right.get("name"))
        and float(left.get("max_offset", np.nan)) == float(right.get("max_offset", np.nan))
    )


def _solver_imperfection_records(raw_result: Any) -> Optional[List[Mapping[str, Any]]]:
    info = getattr(raw_result, "info", None)
    if not isinstance(info, Mapping) or "imperfection" not in info:
        return None
    return _records(info.get("imperfection"))


class SolvedGeometry:
    """The FE model a result's displacements are measured from, or a refusal."""

    __slots__ = ("_model", "_refusal")

    def __init__(self, *, model: Any = None, refusal: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
        if (model is None) == (refusal is None):
            raise ValueError("SolvedGeometry needs exactly one of a model or a refusal")
        self._model = model
        self._refusal = refusal

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @classmethod
    def exact(cls, model: Any) -> "SolvedGeometry":
        """Bind to the model object the solver returned as solved."""

        return cls(model=model)

    @classmethod
    def refused(cls, reason: str) -> "SolvedGeometry":
        """A binding that refuses recovery, e.g. for views of an unbound result."""

        return cls(refusal=str(reason))

    @classmethod
    def rebuild(cls, base_model: Any, imperfection_field: Any, raw_result: Any,
                *, context: str) -> Optional["SolvedGeometry"]:
        """Bind to ``base_model`` plus the run-time imperfection the solver applied.

        Call this straight after the solve, with the owned snapshot from
        :func:`owned_imperfection_field` that was also the object handed to the
        solver -- so solve and recovery consume one conversion.  The copy is
        made here, eagerly, so later edits to the built model (coordinates,
        thickness, materials, connectivity) cannot leak into it: it is the same
        deep copy of the same model state the solver made, moved by the same
        offsets through the same :func:`apply_imperfection`.  Returns ``None``
        when every offset is exactly zero: the solved coordinates are then the
        base coordinates bit for bit, and the ordinary route stays untouched.
        """

        from anyfesolver.imperfections import ImperfectionField

        if not isinstance(imperfection_field, ImperfectionField):
            raise TypeError("rebuild needs the owned ImperfectionField given to the solver")
        records = _solver_imperfection_records(raw_result)
        build_records = _records(getattr(base_model, "imperfection_metadata", None))
        expected = {"name": imperfection_field.name, "max_offset": imperfection_field.max_offset}
        if (
            records is None
            or len(records) != len(build_records) + 1
            or not all(_same_record(a, b) for a, b in zip(records, build_records))
            or not _same_record(records[-1], expected)
        ):
            raise UnboundSolvedGeometryError(
                f"{context}: the solver's imperfection record {records!r} does not "
                f"match the build-time imperfections {build_records!r} plus the "
                f"run-time imperfection {expected!r}; the solved geometry cannot be "
                "reproduced, so no stresses are recovered on it"
            )
        if not _has_offset(imperfection_field):
            return None
        from anyfesolver import apply_imperfection

        return cls(model=apply_imperfection(base_model, imperfection_field, copy_model=True))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def model(self) -> Any:
        """The solved FE model; raises when this binding is a refusal."""

        if self._refusal is not None:
            raise UnboundSolvedGeometryError(self._refusal)
        return self._model

    def __getstate__(self):
        return (self._model, self._refusal)

    def __setstate__(self, state) -> None:
        self._model, self._refusal = state


def _runtime_imperfection_unbound(view: Any) -> Optional[str]:
    records = _solver_imperfection_records(getattr(view, "raw_result", None))
    if records is not None:
        build_records = _records(getattr(view.built.fe_model, "imperfection_metadata", None))
        runtime = records[len(build_records):]
        if any(float(entry.get("max_offset", np.nan)) != 0.0 for entry in runtime):
            return "a run-time imperfection"
    info = getattr(view, "info", None)
    workflow = info.get("raw") if isinstance(info, Mapping) else None
    imperfect = getattr(workflow, "imperfect_model", None)
    if imperfect is not None and imperfect is not view.built.fe_model:
        field = getattr(workflow, "imperfection", None)
        if field is None or _has_offset(field):
            return "the capacity workflow's imperfection"
    return None


def unbound_refusal(view: Any) -> Optional[str]:
    """The refusal message for an imperfect result without a binding, if any."""

    reason = _runtime_imperfection_unbound(view)
    if reason is None:
        return None
    return (
        f"this result was solved on geometry moved by {reason}, but it carries "
        "no binding to that solved model; stresses recovered on the perfect "
        "built model would be wrong, so recovery is refused"
    )


def solved_model_of(view: Any) -> Any:
    """The FE model to recover ``view``'s displacements on.

    Views without a binding use ``built.fe_model``, exactly as before -- unless
    their solver record says the solve ran on imperfect geometry that is not
    in ``built.fe_model``, which is refused rather than recovered on the
    perfect shape.
    """

    geometry = getattr(view, "solved_geometry", None)
    if geometry is not None:
        return geometry.model()
    refusal = unbound_refusal(view)
    if refusal is not None:
        raise UnboundSolvedGeometryError(refusal)
    return view.built.fe_model


def solved_reference_positions(view: Any) -> Optional[np.ndarray]:
    """Reference coordinates of a result solved on moved geometry, else ``None``.

    ``None`` means the ordinary route: the mesh coordinates are the reference,
    untouched.  Otherwise the rows follow ``sorted(built.mesh.nodes)`` and come
    from the bound solved model; a refused or missing binding raises.
    """

    geometry = getattr(view, "solved_geometry", None)
    if geometry is None:
        refusal = unbound_refusal(view)
        if refusal is not None:
            raise UnboundSolvedGeometryError(refusal)
        return None
    model = geometry.model()
    get_node = model.mesh.get_node
    rows = []
    for node_id in sorted(view.built.mesh.nodes):
        node = get_node(node_id)
        if node is None:
            raise UnboundSolvedGeometryError(
                f"the solved model has no node {node_id}; its geometry does not match the mesh"
            )
        rows.append((node.x, node.y, node.z))
    return np.asarray(rows, dtype=float).reshape(-1, 3)


def reference_position_map(view: Any, mesh: Any) -> Any:
    """``node id -> xyz`` of the reference geometry: ``mesh.nodes`` unless moved."""

    positions = solved_reference_positions(view)
    if positions is None:
        return mesh.nodes
    return {node_id: positions[row] for row, node_id in enumerate(sorted(mesh.nodes))}
"""Display, probes and saved artifacts use the geometry the solve ran on.

A run-time (or capacity-workflow) imperfection moves the reference coordinates
of a copy of the model; the displacements are measured from that copy.  The
deformed shape must therefore be "imperfect + displacement", and a saved
artifact must carry that reference so reopening it draws the same shape.
Runs without an imperfection, and project imperfections applied at build time,
keep drawing on the mesh coordinates bit for bit.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from anyfem import eigenmode_imperfection, solve_capacity, solve_nonlinear_static
from anyfem.io.artifacts import ArtifactStore
from anyfem.io.result_artifact import write_solution_artifact
from anyfem.ui.scene import build_persisted_result_scene, build_result_scene

from test_imperfect_solved_geometry import (  # noqa: F401
    AMPLITUDE,
    compressed_plate,
    plate_and_mode,
)


def _fe_coordinates(model, node_ids):
    return np.array([[n.x, n.y, n.z] for n in (model.mesh.get_node(i) for i in node_ids)])


def _scene_positions(scene):
    return np.concatenate(
        [np.asarray(p, dtype=float).reshape(-1, 3) for patch in scene.faces for p in patch.polygons]
    )


def _runtime(plate_and_mode):
    project, mesh, buckling = plate_and_mode
    imperfection = eigenmode_imperfection(buckling, 1, amplitude=AMPLITUDE)
    return solve_nonlinear_static(
        project, mesh=mesh, imperfection=imperfection, max_load_factor=1.0, num_steps=1,
    )


def _capacity(plate_and_mode):
    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
    return solve_capacity(
        project, mesh=mesh, num_buckling_modes=1, imperfection_amplitude=AMPLITUDE,
        num_steps=1, max_load_factor=1.0,
    )


def _store(tmp_path, solution):
    project = solution.built.project
    store = ArtifactStore(tmp_path / "plate.anyfem")
    identity = dict(mesh_id="mesh", document_id=project.document_id,
                    model_hash="model", mesh_hash="hash")
    store.write_mesh(solution.built.mesh, **identity)
    artifact = write_solution_artifact(store, solution, job_id="job", analysis_hash="a", **identity)
    return store, artifact


@pytest.mark.parametrize("route", ["nonlinear", "capacity"])
def test_deformed_positions_are_imperfect_reference_plus_displacement(plate_and_mode, route):
    solution = (_runtime if route == "nonlinear" else _capacity)(plate_and_mode)
    ids = sorted(solution.built.mesh.nodes)
    reference = _fe_coordinates(solution.solved_model(), ids)
    assert np.max(np.abs(reference - solution.built.mesh.node_positions())) > 0.5 * AMPLITUDE

    np.testing.assert_array_equal(solution.reference_positions(), reference)
    expected = reference + 3.0 * solution.translations()
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(solution.deformed_positions(3.0), expected)

    scene = build_result_scene(solution, field="magnitude", scale=3.0)
    by_node = dict(zip(ids, expected))
    first = next(iter(solution.built.mesh.shells))
    corners = np.array([by_node[n] for n in solution.built.mesh.corners_of(first)])
    polygons = [np.asarray(poly) for patch in scene.faces for poly in patch.polygons]
    assert any(poly.shape == corners.shape and np.array_equal(poly, corners) for poly in polygons)


def test_csv_positions_use_the_reference_geometry(plate_and_mode):
    from anyfem.post.report import field_to_csv

    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
    ids = sorted(solution.built.mesh.nodes)
    reference = dict(zip(ids, _fe_coordinates(solution.solved_model(), ids)))
    rows = [line.split(",") for line in field_to_csv(solution, "magnitude").splitlines()[1:]]
    assert rows
    for row in rows:
        np.testing.assert_allclose([float(v) for v in row[1:4]], reference[int(row[0])], atol=1e-8)


def test_artifact_records_the_reference_geometry_and_reopens_on_it(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, solution)
    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)
    ids = sorted(solution.built.mesh.nodes)
    reference = _fe_coordinates(solution.solved_model(), ids)

    assert dataset.metadata("provenance")["reference_geometry"]["kind"] == "solved_imperfect"
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(np.asarray(dataset.table("reference_node_ids")), ids)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(np.asarray(dataset.table("reference_node_positions")), reference)

    scene = build_persisted_result_scene(
        solution.built.project, solution.built.mesh, dataset, "displacement", scale=3.0
    )
    live = build_result_scene(solution, field="magnitude", scale=3.0)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(_scene_positions(scene), _scene_positions(live))


def test_ordinary_artifact_names_the_mesh_and_gains_no_table(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
    solution = solve_nonlinear_static(project, mesh=mesh, max_load_factor=0.5, num_steps=1)
    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, solution)
    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)
    assert dataset.metadata("provenance")["reference_geometry"]["kind"] == "mesh_record"
    assert "reference_node_positions" not in dataset.table_keys
    scene = build_persisted_result_scene(
        project, solution.built.mesh, dataset, "displacement", scale=3.0
    )
    live = build_result_scene(solution, field="magnitude", scale=3.0)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(_scene_positions(scene), _scene_positions(live))


def test_artifact_without_a_reference_record_reads_as_the_mesh(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
    from anyfem.ui.scene import persisted_reference_positions

    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
    solution = solve_nonlinear_static(project, mesh=mesh, max_load_factor=0.5, num_steps=1)
    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, solution)
    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)

    class Old:  # an artifact written before the reference record existed
        table_keys = tuple(k for k in dataset.table_keys if not k.startswith("reference_"))
        table = staticmethod(dataset.table)

        @staticmethod
        def metadata(name):
            old = dict(dataset.metadata(name))
            old.pop("reference_geometry", None)
            return old

    ordered = sorted(mesh.nodes)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(
        persisted_reference_positions(Old, mesh, ordered), mesh.node_positions()
    )


def test_unbound_runtime_imperfection_refuses_the_display(plate_and_mode):
    from anyfem.post.results import NonlinearSolution, UnboundSolvedGeometryError

    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
    unbound = NonlinearSolution(
        displacements=solution.displacements, built=solution.built,
        raw_result=solution.raw_result, info=dict(solution.info),
    )
    with pytest.raises(UnboundSolvedGeometryError):
        unbound.deformed_positions(1.0)


def test_ordinary_and_build_time_displays_are_unchanged(plate_and_mode):
    from anyfem.model.imperfections import Imperfection

    project, mesh, _ = plate_and_mode
    plain = solve_nonlinear_static(project, mesh=mesh, max_load_factor=0.5, num_steps=1)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(
        plain.deformed_positions(2.0), mesh.node_positions() + 2.0 * plain.translations()
    )
    p2, face = compressed_plate()
    p2.imperfections.append(Imperfection(name="dent", ref=p2.face(face), amplitude=AMPLITUDE,
                                         kind="plate_mode", direction=(0.0, 0.0, 1.0)))
    m2 = p2.generate_mesh(0.25)
    built_time = solve_nonlinear_static(p2, mesh=m2, max_load_factor=0.5, num_steps=1)
    # Pinned scope: build-time imperfections keep drawing on the mesh record.
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(
        built_time.deformed_positions(2.0), m2.node_positions() + 2.0 * built_time.translations()
    )


def test_artifact_of_an_unbound_imperfect_result_refuses_its_reference(plate_and_mode, tmp_path):
    from anyfem.post.results import NonlinearSolution

    solution = _runtime(plate_and_mode)
    unbound = NonlinearSolution(
        displacements=solution.displacements, built=solution.built,
        raw_result=solution.raw_result, info=dict(solution.info),
    )
    store, artifact = _store(tmp_path, unbound)
    dataset = store.open_result(artifact)
    assert dataset.metadata("provenance")["reference_geometry"]["kind"] == "unbound_imperfect"
    assert "reference_node_positions" not in dataset.table_keys
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="does not store"):
        build_persisted_result_scene(
            unbound.built.project, unbound.built.mesh, dataset, "displacement", scale=1.0
        )

def write_report(
    solution, path: str | Path, *, fields: Sequence[str] = _DEFAULT_FIELDS
) -> Path:
    """Write the Markdown summary to a file."""

    destination = Path(path)
    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    destination.write_text(
        report_markdown(solution, fields=fields), encoding="utf-8"
    )
    return destination


def field_to_csv(shape, name: str = "magnitude", **kwargs: Any) -> str:
    """One field as CSV, with the position of each node or element."""

    from .fields import element_centroids

    evaluated = evaluate_field(shape, name, **kwargs)
    mesh = shape.built.mesh
    unit = evaluated.unit or field_unit(name)
    header = f"id,x,y,z,{name}" + (f"_{unit}" if unit else "")

    if evaluated.per_element:
        centroids = element_centroids(mesh)
        rows = [
            (key, centroids[key], value)
            for key, value in sorted(evaluated.element_values.items())
            if key in centroids
        ]
    else:
        from .solved_geometry import reference_position_map

        coordinates = reference_position_map(shape, mesh)
        rows = [
            (key, coordinates[key], value)
            for key, value in sorted(evaluated.node_values.items())
        ]

    body = [
        f"{key},{point[0]:.9g},{point[1]:.9g},{point[2]:.9g},{value:.9g}"
        for key, point, value in rows
    ]
    return "\n".join([header, *body])


def path_to_csv(path_result: PathResult) -> str:
    return path_result.to_csv()


def write_csv(text_or_result: Any, path: str | Path) -> Path:
    """Write CSV text, or a PathResult, to a file."""

    destination = Path(path)
    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    text = (
        text_or_result.to_csv()
        if isinstance(text_or_result, PathResult)
        else str(text_or_result)
    )
    destination.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    return destination


def _first_shape(solution):
    shapes = getattr(solution, "shapes", None)
    if shapes:
        return shapes[0]
    return solution

"""Adapt displayable ANYfem solutions to immutable result sidecars.

The postprocessing wrappers deliberately present a small, convenient surface,
while solver results retain analysis-specific histories.  This module is the
single boundary between those two contracts and :meth:`ArtifactStore.write_result`.
It writes only quantities that are actually present: an imported stress-only
result does not acquire a zero displacement field, and an envelope-only
transient does not acquire invented time frames.

All field arrays are frame-major.  Even a static field has a leading frame
axis, so ``ResultField.read(0)`` always returns the complete spatial field.
Entity IDs are stored alongside each spatial field in ``tables`` because the
v1 result sidecar has no built-in association dataset.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field, is_dataclass
from enum import Enum
from importlib import metadata as importlib_metadata
import math
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import re
from typing import Any, Mapping, Optional, Sequence

import numpy as np

from ..model.records import ArtifactRef, ResultQuantityDescriptor
from ..post.solver_data import available_solution_quantities
from .artifacts import ArtifactStore

__all__ = [
    "ResultArtifactPayload",
    "build_result_artifact_inputs",
    "result_artifact_payload",
    "write_solution_artifact",
]


_DOF_COMPONENTS = ("ux", "uy", "uz", "rx", "ry", "rz")
_VECTOR_COMPONENTS = ("x", "y", "z")
_GLOBAL_SURFACE_STRESSES = frozenset(
    f"global_{component}_{surface}"
    for component in ("xx", "yy", "zz", "xy", "xz", "yz")
    for surface in ("top", "bot")
)
_SAFE_KEY = re.compile(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]+")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ResultArtifactPayload:
    """The analysis-dependent portion of ``ArtifactStore.write_result``.

    Identity and hash arguments are intentionally absent.  They belong to the
    immutable job submission and are supplied by :func:`write_solution_artifact`.
    """

    fields: Mapping[str, tuple[ResultQuantityDescriptor, Any]] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )
    frames: tuple[float, ...] = ()
    frame_kind: str = "static"
    histories: Mapping[str, tuple[Sequence[float], Sequence[float]]] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )
    tables: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    provenance: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    summary: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    diagnostics: tuple[Any, ...] = ()
    partial: bool = False

    def write_result_inputs(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Return fresh keyword arguments accepted by ``write_result``."""

        return {
            "fields": dict(self.fields),
            "frames": tuple(self.frames),
            "frame_kind": self.frame_kind,
            "histories": dict(self.histories),
            "tables": dict(self.tables),
            "provenance": dict(self.provenance),
            "summary": dict(self.summary),
            "diagnostics": tuple(self.diagnostics),
            "partial": bool(self.partial),
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class _PackedDofs:
    values: np.ndarray
    node_ids: tuple[int, ...]
    components: tuple[str, ...]
    dof_indices: tuple[int, ...]
    rectangular: bool


class _Builder:
    def __init__(self, *, frames: Sequence[float], frame_kind: str) -> None:
        self.frames = tuple(float(value) for value in frames)
        self.frame_kind = str(frame_kind)
        self.fields: dict[str, tuple[ResultQuantityDescriptor, Any]] = {}
        self.histories: dict[str, tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]] = {}
        self.tables: dict[str, Any] = {}

    def key(self, value: object) -> str:
        base = _safe_key(value)
        candidate = base
        index = 2
        while candidate in self.fields:
            candidate = f"{base}_{index}"
            index += 1
        return candidate

    def add_field(
        self,
        key: str,
        values: Any,
        *,
        label: str,
        location: str,
        unit: str = "",
        components: Sequence[str] = (),
        basis: str = "global",
        frames: Sequence[float] = (),
        recovery: str = "native",
        reduction: str = "none",
        deformation_required: bool = False,
        provenance: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> Optional[str]:
        array = _numeric_array(values)
        if array is None or array.size == 0:
            return None
        # A scalar cannot be compressed/chunked by the v1 artifact writer.
        if array.ndim == 0:
            array = array.reshape(1)
        resolved = self.key(key)
        descriptor = ResultQuantityDescriptor(
            key=resolved,
            label=str(label),
            location=location,
            unit=str(unit),
            components=tuple(str(value) for value in components),
            basis=str(basis),
            frames=tuple(float(value) for value in frames),
            recovery=str(recovery),
            reduction=str(reduction),
            deformation_required=bool(deformation_required),
            provenance=_json_safe(dict(provenance or {})),
        )
        self.fields[resolved] = (descriptor, array)
        return resolved

    def add_history(
        self,
        key: str,
        x_values: Any,
        y_values: Any,
    ) -> None:
        x = _numeric_array(x_values)
        y = _numeric_array(y_values)
        if x is None or y is None:
            return
        x = x.reshape(-1)
        y = y.reshape(-1)
        if x.size == 0 or x.shape != y.shape:
            return
        self.histories[_safe_key(key)] = (x, y)

    def add_table(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
        if value is None:
            return
        safe_key = _safe_key(key)
        if isinstance(value, np.ndarray) and value.dtype.kind not in "OUS":
            self.tables[safe_key] = value.reshape(1) if value.ndim == 0 else value
            return
        safe = _json_safe(value)
        try:
            array = np.asarray(safe)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            # The artifact writer probes tables with np.asarray before choosing
            # JSON.  A wrapper object keeps ragged contact/state histories on
            # the JSON path instead of failing that probe.
            self.tables[safe_key] = {"values": safe}
            return
        if array.ndim == 0 and array.dtype.kind in "biufc":
            self.tables[safe_key] = array.reshape(1)
        else:
            self.tables[safe_key] = safe


def result_artifact_payload(
    solution: Any,
    *,
    provenance: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,
    summary: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,
    diagnostics: Sequence[Any] = (),
    partial: bool = False,
) -> ResultArtifactPayload:
    """Describe all persistable quantities actually present on ``solution``.

    The function accepts the current linear, modal, buckling, nonlinear,
    capacity, transient, impact and imported wrappers.  It is intentionally
    duck-typed at the solver-result boundary so newer raw result bundles can be
    retained without making this module depend on their concrete classes.
    """

    if solution is None:
        raise TypeError("solution must not be None")

    kind = type(solution).__name__
    frame_values, frame_kind = _primary_frames(solution)
    builder = _Builder(frames=frame_values, frame_kind=frame_kind)

    if kind == "ImportedSolution":
        _adapt_imported(builder, solution)
    elif kind == "ImpactSolution":
        _adapt_transient(builder, solution, impact=True)
    elif kind == "TransientSolution":
        _adapt_transient(builder, solution, impact=False)
    elif kind == "CapacitySolution":
        _adapt_nonlinear(builder, solution, capacity=True)
    elif kind == "NonlinearSolution":
        _adapt_nonlinear(builder, solution, capacity=False)
    elif kind == "ModalSolution":
        _adapt_modes(builder, solution, modal=True)
    elif kind == "BucklingSolution":
        _adapt_modes(builder, solution, modal=False)
    elif kind == "LinearBatchSolution":
        _adapt_linear_batch(builder, solution)
    elif kind == "LinearSolution" or hasattr(solution, "displacements"):
        _adapt_linear(builder, solution)
    else:
        raise TypeError(f"unsupported ANYfem solution wrapper {kind!r}")

    raw = _raw_result(solution)
    _add_common_raw_quantities(builder, raw, solution)
    global_stress = getattr(solution, "_requested_global_stress", None)
    if global_stress is not None:
        _add_static_stresses(builder, global_stress, prefix="stress", global_only=True,
                             frames=_recovery_frames(global_stress))
    patch_stress = getattr(solution, "_requested_patch_stress", None)
    if patch_stress is not None:
        _add_patch_stresses(builder, (patch_stress,), _recovery_frames(patch_stress))
    if "stress_global_xx_top" in builder.fields:
        from ..post.results import submitted_plate_thicknesses
        thicknesses = submitted_plate_thicknesses(solution.built)
        if thicknesses:
            builder.add_table("shell_face_thickness_m", thicknesses)
    reference_geometry = _add_reference_geometry(builder, solution)
    outcome = _solution_outcome(solution, raw)

    resolved_solver_quantities = (
        ()
        if kind == "ImportedSolution"
        else available_solution_quantities(
            solution,
            recovered=getattr(solution, "_stress", None),
        )
    )
    _add_canonical_solver_quantities(builder, resolved_solver_quantities)

    built_provenance = _base_provenance(solution, raw)
    built_provenance["reference_geometry"] = reference_geometry
    if outcome is not None:
        built_provenance["outcome"] = _json_safe(outcome.to_dict())
    built_provenance["solver_quantity_descriptors"] = [
        item.descriptor.to_dict() for item in resolved_solver_quantities
    ]
    built_provenance.update(_json_safe(dict(provenance or {})))
    built_summary = _base_summary(solution, builder)
    if outcome is not None:
        encoded_outcome = _json_safe(outcome.to_dict())
        built_summary["outcome"] = encoded_outcome
        built_summary["termination"] = str(outcome.termination)
    built_summary.update(_json_safe(dict(summary or {})))
    built_diagnostics = _collect_diagnostics(solution, raw, diagnostics)

    payload = ResultArtifactPayload(
        fields=dict(builder.fields),

codex
