1877:def _layout(solution: Any) -> tuple[tuple[int, ...], Any, int]:
    reduction: str = "mean",
    stresses: Any = None,
) -> Field:
    """Evaluate one named field over a shape.

    ``stresses`` lets a caller reuse a recovery it already has, which matters
    when sweeping every step of a transient.

    A name this module does not label is still evaluated if the solver's
    recovery reports it.  The named lists here exist to give fields a unit and
    a menu entry, not to gate what the solver is allowed to produce: recovery
    grows components over time, and a probe that asks for everything the solver
    knows must not fail merely because this list is a version behind it.
    """

    # A result read from another solver's file carries its stresses already
    # computed. They are looked up before anything else and never recovered:
    # there is no displacement field here complete enough to recover from, and
    # recomputing would silently replace the imported answer with this layer's
    # opinion of it.
    from .access import evaluate_shape_field, _RESULT_ACCESS
    access=_RESULT_ACCESS.get()
    if access is not None and stresses is None:
        return access.field(shape,name,reduction=reduction)
    return evaluate_shape_field(shape, name, reduction=reduction, stresses=stresses)


def reported_fields(stresses: Any, shape: Any) -> List[str]:
    """Every element-stress component this particular result actually carries.

    Taken from the recovery rather than from a list here, so a component the
    solver has recently started reporting shows up without ANYfem being
    changed to know its name.

    Entries whose value is not a number are skipped.  Recovery carries its own
    provenance in the same dictionary -- which equivalent-stress measure was
    used, for instance -- and that is a label about the result, not a field
    over it.
    """

    mesh = shape.built.mesh
    names: List[str] = []
    for element_id in list(mesh.shells) + list(mesh.beams):
        stress = stresses.element_stresses.get(element_id)
        if not stress:
            continue
        for name, value in stress.items():
            if name not in names and _is_numeric(value):
                names.append(name)
    return names

def test_capacity_retains_reference_static_and_buckling_stages():
    built = _built()
    nonlinear_raw = SimpleNamespace(snapshots=(), element_states={})
    buckling = BucklingSolution(
        built=built,
        shapes=[ShapeView(_vector(20.0), built, value=2.0)],
    )
    workflow = SimpleNamespace(
        static_displacements=_vector(30.0),
        nonlinear_result=nonlinear_raw,
        diagnostics={"workflow": "complete"},
    )
    solution = CapacitySolution(
        displacements=_vector(40.0),
        built=built,
        value=1.2,
        buckling=buckling,
        raw_result=nonlinear_raw,
        info={"raw": workflow},
    )
    payload = result_artifact_payload(solution)
    assert payload.fields["displacement"][1].shape == (1, 2, 6)
    assert payload.fields["static_displacement"][1].shape == (1, 2, 6)
    assert payload.fields["buckling_mode_shape"][1].shape == (1, 2, 6)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Introspection, metadata and sanitization
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _layout(solution: Any) -> tuple[tuple[int, ...], Any, int]:
    built = getattr(solution, "built", None)
    fe_model = getattr(built, "fe_model", None)
    fe_mesh = getattr(fe_model, "mesh", None)
    manager = getattr(fe_mesh, "dof_manager", None)
    if manager is None:
        raise ValueError("solution has no FE DOF manager")
    mapped_mesh = getattr(built, "mesh", None)
    nodes = getattr(mapped_mesh, "nodes", None)
    if not isinstance(nodes, Mapping):
        nodes = getattr(fe_mesh, "nodes", {})
    node_ids = tuple(sorted(int(value) for value in nodes))
    total_dofs = int(getattr(manager, "total_dofs", 0))
    return node_ids, manager, total_dofs

the solver reported; it does not editorialise about whether the answer is
acceptable, which is the engineer's call.

    solution,

    """A Markdown summary of one result."""

from types import SimpleNamespace, ModuleType
from typing import Any, Mapping, Dict
import sys
import numpy as np

def load_functions(path, names, namespace):
    tree = ast.parse(Path(path).read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    selected = [n for n in tree.body if isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name in names]
    exec(compile(ast.Module(body=selected, type_ignores=[]), path, 'exec'), namespace)

    def __init__(self, record, ids=None, positions=None):
        self.record, self.ids, self.positions = record, ids, positions
    def metadata(self, name):
        return self.record
    def table(self, name):
        return self.ids if name == 'ids' else self.positions

1. **[P2] Element CSV coordinates still use the perfect mesh** — [report.py:151](/C:/ANYworkspaceWork/anyfem-imperfect-display-20261118/src/anyfem/post/report.py:151). Only nodal fields use `reference_position_map`. Exporting element stresses from an imperfect solve still computes centroids from `built.mesh`. In an isolated reproduction with every reference node shifted to `z=0.1`, the element CSV reported `z=0`. Compute element centroids from the bound reference coordinates too.

2. **[P2] Capacity artifacts apply collapse geometry to earlier stages** — [scene.py:1565](/C:/ANYworkspaceWork/anyfem-imperfect-display-20261118/src/anyfem/ui/scene.py:1565). Capacity artifacts also contain `static_displacement` and `buckling_mode_shape`, which belong to the original geometry. The reader unconditionally applies the nonlinear imperfect reference to every field. Reopening either earlier stage therefore shifts its display onto the collapse imperfection—even at scale zero. These fields need stage-specific reference selection.

3. **[P2] Unrecognized reference metadata silently falls back to perfect geometry** — [scene.py:1541](/C:/ANYworkspaceWork/anyfem-imperfect-display-20261118/src/anyfem/ui/scene.py:1541). A present reference record with an unknown kind, missing kind, or invalid type reaches the ordinary-mesh fallback. An unsupported imperfect artifact consequently displays plausible geometry instead of refusing. Legacy compatibility should permit an absent record; malformed or unsupported records should fail closed.

4. **[P2] Stored node associations are accepted without integrity checks** — [scene.py:1531](/C:/ANYworkspaceWork/anyfem-imperfect-display-20261118/src/anyfem/ui/scene.py:1531). IDs are coerced to integers and inserted into a dictionary without uniqueness or row-count validation. For IDs `[10,20,20]`, node 20 silently receives the last coordinate row. Fractional IDs are also truncated. Validate integral unique IDs and matching coordinate dimensions before constructing the map.

The current regression tests miss these cases. Validation used source tracing and isolated function reproductions; the full suite was not run because solver dependencies were unavailable. No files were edited.
